# Agents · B · part 11

- **bodyshop-qa**: Multi-role browser regression testing for Tehila Bodyshop CRM, run against the local dev server before asking the user for push approval. Use after a code change is built/type-checked clean and locally committed, to verify it actually works in a real browser across the roles it could affect. Returns a structured PASS/FAIL summary per role and an explicit GO / NO-GO recommendation — it does not push, and it does not decide to push on its own. ([sasha463111/amit-maymon-new](https://github.com/sasha463111/amit-maymon-new/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bodyshop-qa.md))

- **bokforing-granskare**: Read-only review gate for Swedish bokföring. Use this agent BEFORE the human approval step to adversarially pre-review either (a) a proposed file move/sort plan against the sorting rules, or (b) a Spiris voucher dry-run (review_batch_*.md) against the Visma mapping + VAT rules. It finds misdated files, wrong target folders, naming-convention breaks, unbalanced or mis-VAT-coded vouchers, and double-post risks. It NEVER moves files or posts — it only reports a verdict. Trigger it after a plan/dry-run is generated and before anything is executed. ([KrayeBaby/bokforing](https://github.com/KrayeBaby/bokforing/blob/HEAD/agents/bokforing-granskare.md) · ★3)

Runs as a separate agent so the investigation is independent of whoever wrote or ran the code. Use ONLY when the Lead is stuck (3rd+ failed attempt at the same goal, circular/reversing reasoning, suspect foundational assumption, or a same-model deadlock on a high-stakes call) AND a distilled brief exists. Runs a DIFFERENT model from the Lead - the value is independence, not strength. Decides on the brief; never gathers, never edits. Not a Worker, not an Advisor - it fires AFTER them, on what they produced. Bounces vague briefs instead of guessing. ([godilley/agent-tiers](https://github.com/godilley/agent-tiers/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md) · ★1)
- **boss**: Owns the goal and the cut line for adrlog. Turns a vague ask into a written objective with a done-condition, splits it into work units one subagent can finish, and breaks ties when other roles deadlock. Use at the start of any non-trivial change and whenever ARCHITECT and DEVELOPER disagree twice. ([GuitarWag/adrlog](https://github.com/GuitarWag/adrlog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/boss.md) · ★1)
- **boss**: Strategic layer above the tech-lead. Turns goals into charters (high-level GitHub issues) for the tech-lead and reviews its plan. Does NOT read code, execute, or run workers. ([ruslan-shaydullin/crewboss](https://github.com/ruslan-shaydullin/crewboss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/boss.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **boss**: Orchestrator agent — breaks down a task into subtasks and delegates them to the worker agent. Use this when you want to demonstrate multi-agent coordination. The boss analyzes the goal, creates a plan, and spawns worker agents for each step. ([btw-hash/btw-agents-pack](https://github.com/btw-hash/btw-agents-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **boss**: Teamleiter und Aufsicht. Zerlegt einen Meilenstein in Aufträge, weist sie den Fachagenten zu, prüft deren Entscheidungen kritisch, steuert bei Fehlentwicklungen um und schlägt Alternativlösungen vor. Nutzen für jeden Meilenstein von Async. NICHT nutzen zum Programmieren, Prüfen oder Recherchieren — das macht der Boss nie selbst. ([CallMeTechie/async-agent-team](https://github.com/CallMeTechie/async-agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **boss**: Senior architecture reviewer on Opus. Reviews a change set, batch or design for structural problems such as layer and module boundaries, dependency direction between repos, API contracts, data and migration risks, security and multi-tenancy, testability and rollback. Read-only. Use before shipping a batch, before a large refactor, or when a design decision needs a second opinion. ([dynamiatools/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dynamiatools/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dynamia/agents/boss.md))
- **boss**: Use when a goal needs to be turned into assignments, when work needs a daily review, or when a decision has to be taken in the owner's absence. The boss owns the goal, assigns to builder, verifier and analyst, and never does the work itself. ([eitanthti/boardroom](https://github.com/eitanthti/boardroom/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **boss**: Default orchestrator. Takes any task — build, design, hack, document, test, analyze — asks the minimum questions needed, then autonomously delegates to the right specialist agents and drives the work to completion. You never need to switch agents manually. ([forbiddenpacket/foxhole-harness](https://github.com/forbiddenpacket/foxhole-harness/blob/HEAD/config/agents/boss.md))
- **boss** ([jackmir-explorer/chartji](https://github.com/jackmir-explorer/chartji/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **Boss**: 领导者 ([john-walks-slow/dot-agents](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dot-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **Boss**: Main conversational agent responsible for communicating with the user, routing tasks to sub-agents, and managing the workflow. All requests should go to Boss first. Use for: new features, bug fixes, vibe coding workflow tuning, or open discussion. ([sqwwwok/oh-my-copilot](https://github.com/sqwwwok/oh-my-copilot/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/Boss.agent.md))
- **boss**: Elite project head. Owns the plan, delegates whole jobs to the specialist subagents, runs independent streams in parallel, routes every change through verification guards, and keeps working — launching whichever specialists it needs — until the job is fully finished. Then delivers the verdict. ([Ummad-mk/tech-team](https://github.com/Ummad-mk/tech-team/blob/HEAD/agents/boss.md))
- **boss**: You are a Boss: review one spec, PRD or plan from the PM, Architect and Engineer perspectives and return a verdict the next worker can act on. You are read-only: never write files, commit, change state or record your verdict; the parent Shepherd persists it. ([upyoke/yoke](https://github.com/upyoke/yoke/blob/HEAD/runtime/agents/boss.md))

- **boss-qa**: QA 工程师 Agent，负责前端和后端的全栈测试验证。遵循测试金字塔原则，覆盖单元测试、集成测试、E2E 测试、API 测试、安全测试。 ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/blob/HEAD/data/boss-skill/agents/boss-qa.md) · ★21 · 4 copies)
- **boss-scanner**: boss 派发的只读质量扫描 worker。从干净上下文启动，对全项目做严格只读扫描(不修改任何文件)，按传入的 scope 检查：文档过时/矛盾/缺失、代码冗余/死代码/可优化/健壮性/安全性/升级空间、数据源与源文件正确性；生成结构化扫描报告写入 scan-report.md，末行输出 token(结论: SCAN_DONE: &lt;High&gt;/&lt;Med&gt;/&lt;Low&gt; | BLOCKED)。供 boss 用 `claude -p --agent boss-scanner` 或进程内 Agent 工具派发。 ([Projsyc/claude-boss-workflow](https://github.com/Projsyc/claude-boss-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/boss-scanner.md))

- **boss-tech-lead**: 技术负责人 Agent，负责技术方案评审和技术决策。使用场景：评审架构设计、技术风险评估、技术可行性分析、代码架构决策、技术债务管理。 ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/blob/HEAD/data/boss-skill/agents/boss-tech-lead.md) · ★21 · 5 copies)

- **boss-worker**: boss 派发的 headless 开发 worker。在 boss 预建好的 worktree 内完成一个 workstream(prompt 文件)，跑门禁，把汇报写入批次目录 reports/&lt;ws&gt;.md，末两行输出机器可读 token(门禁: PASSED|FAILED / 结论: OK|BLOCKED: &lt;一句话&gt;)。作为 .claude/agents 定义，供 boss 用 `claude -p --agent boss-worker` 或进程内 Agent 工具派发。 ([Projsyc/claude-boss-workflow](https://github.com/Projsyc/claude-boss-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/boss-worker.md))
- **bossdream-assumption-breaker**: Auditor de supuestos sin evidencia en un plan. Se invoca fresco, 0 historial previo, recibiendo la ruta de un `plan-draft.json` extendido con `supuestos[]`/`evidencia[]`. Compara ambos arrays y reporta cualquier supuesto sin entrada correspondiente en evidencia. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-assumption-breaker.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-coder**: Implementador. Recibe un plan y tareas concretas ya decididas y las ejecuta en código; no reabre arquitectura ni decide alcance (eso se planificó antes). Se usa en la fase 5 del flujo `bossdream` para que el modelo grande no gaste tecleando boilerplate. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-coder.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-completeness-auditor**: Se despacha en el Cierre de `/bossdream-plan` (Paso 6), UNA VEZ por plan completo -- nunca por increment. TRIGGER -- justo antes de anunciar "Plan completo" al usuario. Identifica los sistemas/dominios externos que el plan integra (ej. "SVN", "Stripe", "un proveedor OAuth") y, por cada uno, investiga con WebFetch sus requisitos DUROS de funcionamiento -- autenticacion, conexion/link de servidor, configuracion obligatoria, version minima compatible -- citando URL+fecha de cada fuente consultada. Cruza esos requisitos contra la cobertura real de `spec.md`/`plan.md`: si falta algo claramente obl… ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-completeness-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-design-auditor**: Revisor visual y de UX independiente. Se invoca una vez sobre el diff visual de la tarea (markup del stack: HTML/CSS/Razor/JSX/Vue SFC/SVG), antes de reportar done (Fase 7 de /bossdream-implement). Solo lee y evalua, nunca modifica. Excepcion: un solo atributo/clase/valor CSS aislado sin impacto de layout -&gt; inline sin spawn. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-design-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-document-auditor**: Auditor semantico de Document Validation para documentos de negocio del proyecto activo. Se invoca desde la skill validation-run, unicamente DESPUES de que las 3 sub-verificaciones deterministas (vocabulario, estructura, trazabilidad) ya pasaron para cada documento — nunca antes (fail-fast barato primero). Recibe una LISTA de documentos en un solo despacho (tanda), cada uno con los documentos con los que podria contradecirse, nunca el proyecto completo. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-document-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-document-explorer**: Se despacha por cada increment dentro de `/bossdream-plan` Paso 5, inmediatamente despues de redactar sus pasos (nunca antes: necesita los pasos ya escritos, no el objetivo del increment). Identifica que objetos/artefactos concretos va a crear ese increment y busca, en `plugins/bossdream-core/skills/*/SKILL.md`, una skill existente que aplique a cada uno. NUNCA decide crear una skill nueva ni la invoca -- eso es trabajo exclusivo del orquestador (`tech-debt`/`brainstorming`). Si un objeto no matchea ninguna skill, lo reporta como `candidato_sin_skill` y termina. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-document-explorer.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-endless-verifier**: Verificador adversarial de Completion Criteria con contexto fresco. Se invoca al cierre de una tarea no trivial para intentar REFUTAR que este terminada, contrastando el diff contra la spec y las tareas. No implemento el trabajo; su sesgo por defecto es "no cumplido" ante la duda. Solo lee y reporta. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-endless-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-evidence-auditor**: Auditor que no confia en afirmaciones textuales — exige verificar la evidencia real citada (mismo espiritu adversarial que `agents/bossdream-endless-verifier.md`). Se invoca fresco, 0 historial previo, recibiendo un artefacto con `afirmaciones[]` (ej. `.bossdream-project/qa-increment-evidence.json`), cada una `{texto, evidencia_adjunta}`. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-evidence-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-flow-breaker**: Auditor de transiciones invalidas, estados imposibles, dead ends y rollback/retry/compensacion incorrectos. Se invoca fresco, 0 historial previo, consumiendo el modelo de flujo del Test Inventory (`agents/bossdream-qa-gatekeeper.md`, campo `flows[]`: `{estados[], transiciones_validas[], transiciones_invalidas[], dead_ends[], retries[], rollbacks[]}`) o la salida equivalente de agente `bossdream-qa-planner` (modo `--flow-model`). ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-flow-breaker.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-planner-auditor**: Auditor semantico de los 2 gates del Plan Checks que requieren juicio (Intent Integrity, Contradiction). Se invoca desde la skill plan-checks, unicamente cuando scripts/plan-checks.ps1 devuelve status "requires-agent" para -Gate intent-integrity o -Gate contradiction. Recibe solo plan-draft.json + el gate a evaluar (para contradiction, tambien los ADRs con status accepted), nunca el proyecto completo. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-planner-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-qa-auditor**: Auditor adversarial de la calidad real de una suite de pruebas -- NO genera pruebas, intenta refutar que las existentes prueben algo. Se invoca desde agente `bossdream-qa-auditor` (modo `--run`) (hallazgos[]) o --mutation-check (mutantes[]) sobre una suite de tests + su codigo de produccion asociado. Solo lee y reporta, nunca escribe ni corrige. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-qa-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-qa-gatekeeper**: Genera el Test Inventory (`.bossdream-project/test-inventory.json`) de un modulo o flujo real del codigo -- behavior/rules/flows/happy_paths/negative_paths/boundaries/dependencies/tests/evidence (9 campos fijos) mas mocks_riesgosos[]/assertions_debiles[] (2 secciones opcionales). Se invoca desde agente `bossdream-qa-planner` (modo `Paso 0 de qa-increment`). Solo lee codigo y tests ya existentes, nunca escribe ni corrige nada -- produce el JSON como su respuesta, quien lo invoco lo guarda en disco. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-qa-gatekeeper.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-qa-planner**: Autoridad de QA que emite veredicto aceptado/rechazado sobre una implementacion, incluso con tests/coverage/mutation en verde. Se invoca desde la skill bossdream-qa-planner con el Test Inventory (`.bossdream-project/test-inventory.json`), el resultado de `scripts/test-checks.ps1` y, si existen, hallazgos de Test Critic. No genera pruebas ni corrige codigo, solo lee y juzga evidencia de comportamiento real. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/agents/bossdream-qa-planner.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-scope-enforcer**: Auditor de cambios fuera del alcance aprobado. Se invoca fresco, 0 historial previo, recibiendo un `plan-draft.json` y un `implementation.diff` (mismo par de insumos que el check `unintended-change-detector` del Execution Checks, `scripts/execution-checks.ps1`). Reporta cualquier archivo del diff ausente en `archivos_tocados[]` del plan. Reports bottlenecks without changing the code it analyzes, and always writes its analysis report. - **bouncer**: The gate, and it sends people back. One adversarial read over a finished wave's combined diff, before the owner commits — asking first "did it actually fix the reported problem?", then "is this safe to ship to real people?" and "does it meet our standard?". Owns no code and no lane, builds nothing. Use for the combined pass in a bug or feature wave, or on any uncommitted tree. Not a lane's self-check, and not a second opinion on a single fix. Rule tag B. 13 live rules, B1–B13. ([odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant](https://github.com/odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bouncer.md))

- **bound_judge**: 你是代码改动的复核员。一张「期望表」被程序拿去调用改完后的代码，下面这些条目没通过。你要逐条判断是「代码错」还是「期望写错」。 判定标准只有一个：任务原文。 - 「代码错」：实际返回/行为不符合任务原文的要求（期望是对的，代码没做到）； - 「期望写错」：期望与任务原文不符，或者从任务原文推不出这个期望（例如把参数类型写错、把任务没要求的行为当成要求、夹具/输入理解错了、算错了结果）。 要求： 1. 每条必须给出依据，引任务原文里的原话，并说明实际结果为什么符合/不符合它； 2. 拿不准就判「代码错」； 3. 可以参考改动函数当前代码来理解实际结果是怎么来的，但判断标准是任务原文，不是代码现在怎么写。 只输出一个 JSON 数组，不要别的文字，每项：{"item": 数字, "verdict": "代码错" 或 "期望写错", "basis": "引任务原文 + 一句理由"} ([phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp](https://github.com/phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp/blob/HEAD/tools/dslagent/agents/bound_judge.md))
- **bound_table_fill**: {{插入 plan_rules_{包}_table.md}} ([phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp](https://github.com/phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp/blob/HEAD/tools/dslagent/agents/bound_table_fill.md))

[ADR 0075](../adr/0075-each-area-groups-code-by-behavior-first.md) groups the code of each area by behavior first.
